    12	constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;
    13	constexpr unsigned int MAX_DISTANCE = 200;
    14	constexpr unsigned int TILE_SIZE = 32;
    15	constexpr unsigned int PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS = 16;
    16	constexpr unsigned int LARGE_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS = 8;
    17	constexpr unsigned int SMALL_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS = 16;
    18	constexpr unsigned int SMALL_GRAPH_TILE_LIMIT = 32;
    19	
    20	// The matrix is column-major: row + column * number_of_rows. Keeping the row
    21	// coordinate in threadIdx.x makes every global-memory transaction coalesced.
    22	inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t row, const size_t column,
    23	                             const size_t numNodes) noexcept {
    24	    return column * numNodes + row;
    25	}
    26	
    27	[[noreturn]] void cudaFailure(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
    28	                              const char* file, const int line) {
    29	    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d while evaluating %s: %s\n", file,
    30	                 line, expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
    31	    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
    32	}
    33	
    34	#define CUDA_CHECK(expression)                                                   \
    35	    do {                                                                         \
    36	        const cudaError_t cuda_check_error = (expression);                       \
    37	        if (cuda_check_error != cudaSuccess) {                                   \
    38	            cudaFailure(cuda_check_error, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__);      \
    39	        }                                                                        \
    40	    } while (false)
    41	
    42	void initializeDistanceMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist,
    43	                              const size_t numNodes,
    44	                              const unsigned int rangeMin,
    45	                              const unsigned int rangeMax) {
    46	    unsigned int seed = 42;
    47	    const double range = static_cast<double>(rangeMax - rangeMin) + 1.0;
    48	
    49	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes * numNodes; ++i) {
    50	        dist[i] = rangeMin + static_cast<unsigned int>(
    51	                                 range * rand_r(&seed) /
    52	                                 static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX));
    53	    }
    54	
    55	    for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
    56	        dist[idx2(i, i, numNodes)] = 0;
    57	    }
    58	}
    59	
    60	__device__ __forceinline__ unsigned int matrixIndex(const unsigned int row,
    61	                                                     const unsigned int column,
    62	                                                     const unsigned int n) {
    63	    return column * n + row;
    64	}
    65	
    66	// Phase 1: close the diagonal (pivot) tile for this round.
    67	__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE_SIZE * PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS, 3)
    68	void updatePivotTile(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist, const unsigned int n,
    69	                     const unsigned int round) {
    70	    __shared__ unsigned int pivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
    71	
    72	    const unsigned int localRow = threadIdx.x;
    73	    const unsigned int row = round * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
    74	
    75	#pragma unroll
    76	    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
    77	         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
    78	        const unsigned int column = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
    79	        pivot[localColumn][localRow] =
    80	            (row < n && column < n) ? dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] : INF;
    81	    }
    82	    __syncthreads();
    83	
    84	#pragma unroll
    85	    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
    86	#pragma unroll
    87	        for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
    88	             localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
    89	            const unsigned int oldDistance = pivot[localColumn][localRow];
    90	            const unsigned int throughK =
    91	                pivot[k][localRow] + pivot[localColumn][k];
    92	            if (throughK < oldDistance) {
    93	                pivot[localColumn][localRow] = throughK;
    94	            }
    95	        }
    96	        __syncthreads();
    97	    }
    98	
    99	#pragma unroll
   100	    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
   101	         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
   102	        const unsigned int column = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
   103	        if (row < n && column < n) {
   104	            dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] = pivot[localColumn][localRow];
   105	        }
   106	    }
   107	}
   108	
   109	// Phase 2: update every tile that shares rows or columns with the pivot tile.
   110	// One kernel handles both orientations, halving the number of launches here.
   111	__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE_SIZE * PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS, 3)
   112	void updatePivotRowAndColumn(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
   113	                             const unsigned int n,
   114	                             const unsigned int round,
   115	                             const unsigned int tileCount) {
   116	    __shared__ unsigned int pivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
   117	    __shared__ unsigned int current[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
   118	
   119	    const unsigned int tilesWithoutPivot = tileCount - 1;
   120	    const bool pivotRows = blockIdx.x < tilesWithoutPivot;
   121	    const unsigned int compactTile = blockIdx.x % tilesWithoutPivot;
   122	    const unsigned int otherTile = compactTile + (compactTile >= round);
   123	    const unsigned int rowTile = pivotRows ? round : otherTile;
   124	    const unsigned int columnTile = pivotRows ? otherTile : round;
   125	    const unsigned int localRow = threadIdx.x;
   126	    const unsigned int row = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
   127	    const unsigned int pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
   128	
   129	#pragma unroll
   130	    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
   131	         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
   132	        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
   133	        const unsigned int pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
   134	        current[localColumn][localRow] =
   135	            (row < n && column < n) ? dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] : INF;
   136	        pivot[localColumn][localRow] =
   137	            (pivotRow < n && pivotColumn < n)
   138	                ? dist[matrixIndex(pivotRow, pivotColumn, n)]
   139	                : INF;
   140	    }
   141	    __syncthreads();
   142	
   143	    if (pivotRows) {
   144	#pragma unroll
   145	        for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
   146	#pragma unroll
   147	            for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y;
   148	                 localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
   149	                 localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
   150	                const unsigned int oldDistance = current[localColumn][localRow];
   151	                const unsigned int throughK =
   152	                    pivot[k][localRow] + current[localColumn][k];
   153	                if (throughK < oldDistance) {
   154	                    current[localColumn][localRow] = throughK;
   155	                }
   156	            }
   157	            __syncthreads();
   158	        }
   159	    } else {
   160	#pragma unroll
   161	        for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
   162	#pragma unroll
   163	            for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y;
   164	                 localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
   165	                 localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
   166	                const unsigned int oldDistance = current[localColumn][localRow];
   167	                const unsigned int throughK =
   168	                    current[k][localRow] + pivot[localColumn][k];
   169	                if (throughK < oldDistance) {
   170	                    current[localColumn][localRow] = throughK;
   171	                }
   172	            }
   173	            __syncthreads();
   174	        }
   175	    }
   176	
   177	#pragma unroll
   178	    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
   179	         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
   180	        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
   181	        if (row < n && column < n) {
   182	            dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] = current[localColumn][localRow];
   183	        }
   184	    }
   185	}
   186	
   187	// Phase 3: all non-pivot tiles are independent. The two input tiles are staged
   188	// once in shared memory and each thread keeps multiple outputs in registers.
   189	template <unsigned int BLOCK_ROWS, unsigned int MIN_BLOCKS_PER_SM>
   190	__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE_SIZE * BLOCK_ROWS, MIN_BLOCKS_PER_SM)
   191	void updateRemainingTiles(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
   192	                          const unsigned int n, const unsigned int round) {
   193	    __shared__ unsigned int toPivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
   194	    __shared__ unsigned int fromPivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
   195	
   196	    const unsigned int compactRowTile = blockIdx.x;
   197	    const unsigned int compactColumnTile = blockIdx.y;
   198	    const unsigned int rowTile =
   199	        compactRowTile + (compactRowTile >= round);
   200	    const unsigned int columnTile =
   201	        compactColumnTile + (compactColumnTile >= round);
   202	    const unsigned int localRow = threadIdx.x;
   203	    const unsigned int row = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
   204	    const unsigned int pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
   205	
   206	#pragma unroll
   207	    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
   208	         localColumn += BLOCK_ROWS) {
   209	        const unsigned int pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
   210	        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
   211	        toPivot[localColumn][localRow] =
   212	            (row < n && pivotColumn < n)
   213	                ? dist[matrixIndex(row, pivotColumn, n)]
   214	                : INF;
   215	        fromPivot[localColumn][localRow] =
   216	            (pivotRow < n && column < n)
   217	                ? dist[matrixIndex(pivotRow, column, n)]
   218	                : INF;
   219	    }
   220	    __syncthreads();
   221	
   222	    unsigned int values[TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS];
   223	#pragma unroll
   224	    for (unsigned int item = 0; item < TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS; ++item) {
   225	        const unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y + item * BLOCK_ROWS;
   226	        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
   227	        values[item] = (row < n && column < n)
   228	                           ? dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)]
   229	                           : INF;
   230	    }
   231	
   232	#pragma unroll
   233	    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
   234	        const unsigned int distanceToK = toPivot[k][localRow];
   235	#pragma unroll
   236	        for (unsigned int item = 0; item < TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS; ++item) {
   237	            const unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y + item * BLOCK_ROWS;
   238	            const unsigned int throughK =
   239	                distanceToK + fromPivot[localColumn][k];
   240	            if (throughK < values[item]) {
   241	                values[item] = throughK;
   242	            }
   243	        }
   244	    }
   245	
   246	#pragma unroll
   247	    for (unsigned int item = 0; item < TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS; ++item) {
   248	        const unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y + item * BLOCK_ROWS;
   249	        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
   250	        if (row < n && column < n) {
   251	            dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] = values[item];
   252	        }
   253	    }
   254	}
   255	
   256	void floydWarshall(unsigned int* const deviceDist, const unsigned int numNodes) {
   257	    if (numNodes == 0) {
   258	        return;
   259	    }
   260	
   261	    const unsigned int tileCount =
   262	        (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
   263	    const dim3 phase12Threads(TILE_SIZE, PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS);
   264	
   265	    for (unsigned int round = 0; round < tileCount; ++round) {
   266	        updatePivotTile<<<1, phase12Threads>>>(deviceDist, numNodes, round);
   267	
   268	        if (tileCount > 1) {
   269	            updatePivotRowAndColumn<<<2 * (tileCount - 1), phase12Threads>>>(
   270	                deviceDist, numNodes, round, tileCount);
   271	            const dim3 remainingTiles(tileCount - 1, tileCount - 1);
   272	            if (tileCount <= SMALL_GRAPH_TILE_LIMIT) {
   273	                const dim3 phase3Threads(TILE_SIZE, SMALL_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS);
   274	                updateRemainingTiles<SMALL_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS, 3>
   275	                    <<<remainingTiles, phase3Threads>>>(deviceDist, numNodes,
   276	                                                        round);
   277	            } else {
   278	                const dim3 phase3Threads(TILE_SIZE, LARGE_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS);

 constexpr unsigned int INF = 1000000000;

+// The matrix is column-major: row + column * number_of_rows. Keeping the row
+// coordinate in threadIdx.x makes every global-memory transaction coalesced.
+inline constexpr size_t idx2(const size_t row, const size_t column,
+                             const size_t numNodes) noexcept {

-void initializePathMatrix(std::vector<unsigned int>& path, const size_t numNodes) {

+__device__ __forceinline__ unsigned int matrixIndex(const unsigned int row,
+                                                     const unsigned int column,
+                                                     const unsigned int n) {
+    return column * n + row;
+}
+
+// Phase 1: close the diagonal (pivot) tile for this round.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE_SIZE * PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS, 3)
+void updatePivotTile(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist, const unsigned int n,
+                     const unsigned int round) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const unsigned int localRow = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int row = round * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+        const unsigned int column = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        pivot[localColumn][localRow] =
+            (row < n && column < n) ? dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] : INF;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+             localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+            const unsigned int oldDistance = pivot[localColumn][localRow];
+            const unsigned int throughK =
+                pivot[k][localRow] + pivot[localColumn][k];
+            if (throughK < oldDistance) {
+                pivot[localColumn][localRow] = throughK;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+        const unsigned int column = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        if (row < n && column < n) {
+            dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] = pivot[localColumn][localRow];
         }
-        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
     }
 }
 
-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
-                   const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];

-

-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+// Phase 2: update every tile that shares rows or columns with the pivot tile.
+// One kernel handles both orientations, halving the number of launches here.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE_SIZE * PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS, 3)
+void updatePivotRowAndColumn(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                             const unsigned int n,
+                             const unsigned int round,
+                             const unsigned int tileCount) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int current[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const unsigned int tilesWithoutPivot = tileCount - 1;
+    const bool pivotRows = blockIdx.x < tilesWithoutPivot;
+    const unsigned int compactTile = blockIdx.x % tilesWithoutPivot;
+    const unsigned int otherTile = compactTile + (compactTile >= round);
+    const unsigned int rowTile = pivotRows ? round : otherTile;
+    const unsigned int columnTile = pivotRows ? otherTile : round;
+    const unsigned int localRow = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int row = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
+    const unsigned int pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        const unsigned int pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        current[localColumn][localRow] =
+            (row < n && column < n) ? dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] : INF;
+        pivot[localColumn][localRow] =
+            (pivotRow < n && pivotColumn < n)
+                ? dist[matrixIndex(pivotRow, pivotColumn, n)]
+                : INF;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    if (pivotRows) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+#pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y;
+                 localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+                 localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+                const unsigned int oldDistance = current[localColumn][localRow];
+                const unsigned int throughK =
+                    pivot[k][localRow] + current[localColumn][k];
+                if (throughK < oldDistance) {
+                    current[localColumn][localRow] = throughK;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+    } else {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+#pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y;
+                 localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+                 localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+                const unsigned int oldDistance = current[localColumn][localRow];
+                const unsigned int throughK =
+                    current[k][localRow] + pivot[localColumn][k];
+                if (throughK < oldDistance) {
+                    current[localColumn][localRow] = throughK;
                 }
             }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        if (row < n && column < n) {
+            dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] = current[localColumn][localRow];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Phase 3: all non-pivot tiles are independent. The two input tiles are staged
+// once in shared memory and each thread keeps multiple outputs in registers.
+template <unsigned int BLOCK_ROWS, unsigned int MIN_BLOCKS_PER_SM>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE_SIZE * BLOCK_ROWS, MIN_BLOCKS_PER_SM)
+void updateRemainingTiles(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                          const unsigned int n, const unsigned int round) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int toPivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int fromPivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const unsigned int compactRowTile = blockIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int compactColumnTile = blockIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int rowTile =
+        compactRowTile + (compactRowTile >= round);
+    const unsigned int columnTile =
+        compactColumnTile + (compactColumnTile >= round);
+    const unsigned int localRow = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int row = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
+    const unsigned int pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+         localColumn += BLOCK_ROWS) {
+        const unsigned int pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        toPivot[localColumn][localRow] =
+            (row < n && pivotColumn < n)
+                ? dist[matrixIndex(row, pivotColumn, n)]
+                : INF;
+        fromPivot[localColumn][localRow] =
+            (pivotRow < n && column < n)
+                ? dist[matrixIndex(pivotRow, column, n)]
+                : INF;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    unsigned int values[TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS];
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int item = 0; item < TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS; ++item) {
+        const unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y + item * BLOCK_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        values[item] = (row < n && column < n)
+                           ? dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)]
+                           : INF;
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int distanceToK = toPivot[k][localRow];
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int item = 0; item < TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS; ++item) {
+            const unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y + item * BLOCK_ROWS;
+            const unsigned int throughK =
+                distanceToK + fromPivot[localColumn][k];
+            if (throughK < values[item]) {
+                values[item] = throughK;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int item = 0; item < TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS; ++item) {
+        const unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y + item * BLOCK_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        if (row < n && column < n) {
+            dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] = values[item];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void floydWarshall(unsigned int* const deviceDist, const unsigned int numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const unsigned int tileCount =
+        (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const dim3 phase12Threads(TILE_SIZE, PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS);
+
+    for (unsigned int round = 0; round < tileCount; ++round) {
+        updatePivotTile<<<1, phase12Threads>>>(deviceDist, numNodes, round);
+
+        if (tileCount > 1) {
+            updatePivotRowAndColumn<<<2 * (tileCount - 1), phase12Threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, numNodes, round, tileCount);
+            const dim3 remainingTiles(tileCount - 1, tileCount - 1);
+            if (tileCount <= SMALL_GRAPH_TILE_LIMIT) {
+                const dim3 phase3Threads(TILE_SIZE, SMALL_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS);
+                updateRemainingTiles<SMALL_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS, 3>
+                    <<<remainingTiles, phase3Threads>>>(deviceDist, numNodes,
+                                                        round);
+            } else {
+                const dim3 phase3Threads(TILE_SIZE, LARGE_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS);
+                updateRemainingTiles<LARGE_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS, 4>
+                    <<<remainingTiles, phase3Threads>>>(deviceDist, numNodes,
+                                                        round);
+            }
         }
     }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
 }

+__device__ __forceinline__ unsigned int matrixIndex(const unsigned int row,

+// Phase 1: close the diagonal (pivot) tile for this round.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE_SIZE * PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS, 3)
+void updatePivotTile(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist, const unsigned int n,
+                     const unsigned int round) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const unsigned int localRow = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int row = round * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+        const unsigned int column = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        pivot[localColumn][localRow] =
+            (row < n && column < n) ? dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] : INF;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+             localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+            const unsigned int oldDistance = pivot[localColumn][localRow];
+            const unsigned int throughK =
+                pivot[k][localRow] + pivot[localColumn][k];
+            if (throughK < oldDistance) {
+                pivot[localColumn][localRow] = throughK;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+        const unsigned int column = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        if (row < n && column < n) {
+            dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] = pivot[localColumn][localRow];
         }

     }

-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
-                   const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {

-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {

-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+// Phase 2: update every tile that shares rows or columns with the pivot tile.
+// One kernel handles both orientations, halving the number of launches here.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE_SIZE * PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS, 3)
+void updatePivotRowAndColumn(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                             const unsigned int n,
+                             const unsigned int round,
+                             const unsigned int tileCount) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int current[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const unsigned int tilesWithoutPivot = tileCount - 1;
+    const bool pivotRows = blockIdx.x < tilesWithoutPivot;
+    const unsigned int compactTile = blockIdx.x % tilesWithoutPivot;
+    const unsigned int otherTile = compactTile + (compactTile >= round);
+    const unsigned int rowTile = pivotRows ? round : otherTile;
+    const unsigned int columnTile = pivotRows ? otherTile : round;
+    const unsigned int localRow = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int row = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
+    const unsigned int pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        const unsigned int pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        current[localColumn][localRow] =
+            (row < n && column < n) ? dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] : INF;
+        pivot[localColumn][localRow] =
+            (pivotRow < n && pivotColumn < n)
+                ? dist[matrixIndex(pivotRow, pivotColumn, n)]
+                : INF;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    if (pivotRows) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+#pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y;
+                 localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+                 localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+                const unsigned int oldDistance = current[localColumn][localRow];
+                const unsigned int throughK =
+                    pivot[k][localRow] + current[localColumn][k];
+                if (throughK < oldDistance) {
+                    current[localColumn][localRow] = throughK;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+    } else {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+#pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y;
+                 localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+                 localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+                const unsigned int oldDistance = current[localColumn][localRow];
+                const unsigned int throughK =
+                    current[k][localRow] + pivot[localColumn][k];
+                if (throughK < oldDistance) {
+                    current[localColumn][localRow] = throughK;
                 }
             }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        if (row < n && column < n) {
+            dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] = current[localColumn][localRow];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Phase 3: all non-pivot tiles are independent. The two input tiles are staged
+// once in shared memory and each thread keeps multiple outputs in registers.
+template <unsigned int BLOCK_ROWS, unsigned int MIN_BLOCKS_PER_SM>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE_SIZE * BLOCK_ROWS, MIN_BLOCKS_PER_SM)
+void updateRemainingTiles(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                          const unsigned int n, const unsigned int round) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int toPivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int fromPivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const unsigned int compactRowTile = blockIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int compactColumnTile = blockIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int rowTile =
+        compactRowTile + (compactRowTile >= round);
+    const unsigned int columnTile =
+        compactColumnTile + (compactColumnTile >= round);
+    const unsigned int localRow = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int row = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
+    const unsigned int pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+         localColumn += BLOCK_ROWS) {
+        const unsigned int pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        toPivot[localColumn][localRow] =
+            (row < n && pivotColumn < n)
+                ? dist[matrixIndex(row, pivotColumn, n)]
+                : INF;
+        fromPivot[localColumn][localRow] =
+            (pivotRow < n && column < n)
+                ? dist[matrixIndex(pivotRow, column, n)]
+                : INF;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    unsigned int values[TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS];
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int item = 0; item < TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS; ++item) {
+        const unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y + item * BLOCK_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        values[item] = (row < n && column < n)
+                           ? dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)]
+                           : INF;
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int distanceToK = toPivot[k][localRow];
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int item = 0; item < TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS; ++item) {
+            const unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y + item * BLOCK_ROWS;
+            const unsigned int throughK =
+                distanceToK + fromPivot[localColumn][k];
+            if (throughK < values[item]) {
+                values[item] = throughK;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int item = 0; item < TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS; ++item) {
+        const unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y + item * BLOCK_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        if (row < n && column < n) {
+            dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] = values[item];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void floydWarshall(unsigned int* const deviceDist, const unsigned int numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const unsigned int tileCount =
+        (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const dim3 phase12Threads(TILE_SIZE, PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS);
+
+    for (unsigned int round = 0; round < tileCount; ++round) {
+        updatePivotTile<<<1, phase12Threads>>>(deviceDist, numNodes, round);
+
+        if (tileCount > 1) {
+            updatePivotRowAndColumn<<<2 * (tileCount - 1), phase12Threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, numNodes, round, tileCount);
+            const dim3 remainingTiles(tileCount - 1, tileCount - 1);
+            if (tileCount <= SMALL_GRAPH_TILE_LIMIT) {
+                const dim3 phase3Threads(TILE_SIZE, SMALL_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS);
+                updateRemainingTiles<SMALL_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS, 3>
+                    <<<remainingTiles, phase3Threads>>>(deviceDist, numNodes,
+                                                        round);
+            } else {
+                const dim3 phase3Threads(TILE_SIZE, LARGE_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS);
+                updateRemainingTiles<LARGE_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS, 4>
+                    <<<remainingTiles, phase3Threads>>>(deviceDist, numNodes,
+                                                        round);
+            }
         }
     }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
 }

codex
Implemented an unconditional CUDA version while preserving the `floydwarshall` executable name and adding no files or dependencies.

Key changes:

- Three-phase blocked Floyd–Warshall with 32×32 shared-memory tiles.
- Coalesced column-major memory access and register-resident outputs.
- Adaptive launch geometry for small and large graphs.
- Native GPU architecture optimization.
- CUDA allocation, synchronization, transfer, and error handling.
- Handles partial tiles and empty graphs correctly.

Files changed:

- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/floydwarshall_gpt-5.6-sol-xhigh_cuda_r1/floydwarshall/CMakeLists.txt:1)

Verification:

- Clean CUDA build with `nvcc 12.0`.
- Exact CPU/GPU result hashes matched across numerous sizes, including tile boundaries.
- Built-in validation passed through 8,192 nodes.
- RTX 3090 performance: approximately 192 GOPS at 512 nodes and 2.68 TOPS at 8,192 nodes.

+__device__ __forceinline__ unsigned int matrixIndex(const unsigned int row,
+                                                     const unsigned int column,
+                                                     const unsigned int n) {
+    return column * n + row;

+
+// Phase 1: close the diagonal (pivot) tile for this round.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE_SIZE * PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS, 3)
+void updatePivotTile(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist, const unsigned int n,
+                     const unsigned int round) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const unsigned int localRow = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int row = round * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+        const unsigned int column = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        pivot[localColumn][localRow] =
+            (row < n && column < n) ? dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] : INF;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+             localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+            const unsigned int oldDistance = pivot[localColumn][localRow];
+            const unsigned int throughK =
+                pivot[k][localRow] + pivot[localColumn][k];
+            if (throughK < oldDistance) {
+                pivot[localColumn][localRow] = throughK;
+            }
+        }
+        __syncthreads();
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+        const unsigned int column = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        if (row < n && column < n) {
+            dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] = pivot[localColumn][localRow];
         }
-        path[idx2(j, j, numNodes)] = j;
     }
 }
 
-void floydWarshall(std::vector<unsigned int>& dist, 
-                   std::vector<unsigned int>& path, 
-                   const size_t numNodes) {
-    // Classic Floyd-Warshall algorithm
-    // For each intermediate node k
-    for (size_t k = 0; k < numNodes; ++k) {
-        // For each source node i
-        for (size_t i = 0; i < numNodes; ++i) {
-            // For each destination node j
-            for (size_t j = 0; j < numNodes; ++j) {
-                const unsigned int distIJ = dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)];
-                const unsigned int distIK = dist[idx2(k, i, numNodes)];

-

-                
-                if (newDist < distIJ) {
-                    dist[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = newDist;
-                    path[idx2(j, i, numNodes)] = k;
+// Phase 2: update every tile that shares rows or columns with the pivot tile.
+// One kernel handles both orientations, halving the number of launches here.
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE_SIZE * PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS, 3)
+void updatePivotRowAndColumn(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                             const unsigned int n,
+                             const unsigned int round,
+                             const unsigned int tileCount) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int pivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int current[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const unsigned int tilesWithoutPivot = tileCount - 1;
+    const bool pivotRows = blockIdx.x < tilesWithoutPivot;
+    const unsigned int compactTile = blockIdx.x % tilesWithoutPivot;
+    const unsigned int otherTile = compactTile + (compactTile >= round);
+    const unsigned int rowTile = pivotRows ? round : otherTile;
+    const unsigned int columnTile = pivotRows ? otherTile : round;
+    const unsigned int localRow = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int row = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
+    const unsigned int pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        const unsigned int pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        current[localColumn][localRow] =
+            (row < n && column < n) ? dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] : INF;
+        pivot[localColumn][localRow] =
+            (pivotRow < n && pivotColumn < n)
+                ? dist[matrixIndex(pivotRow, pivotColumn, n)]
+                : INF;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    if (pivotRows) {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+#pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y;
+                 localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+                 localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+                const unsigned int oldDistance = current[localColumn][localRow];
+                const unsigned int throughK =
+                    pivot[k][localRow] + current[localColumn][k];
+                if (throughK < oldDistance) {
+                    current[localColumn][localRow] = throughK;
+                }
+            }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+    } else {
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+#pragma unroll
+            for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y;
+                 localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+                 localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+                const unsigned int oldDistance = current[localColumn][localRow];
+                const unsigned int throughK =
+                    current[k][localRow] + pivot[localColumn][k];
+                if (throughK < oldDistance) {
+                    current[localColumn][localRow] = throughK;
                 }
             }
+            __syncthreads();
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+         localColumn += PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS) {
+        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        if (row < n && column < n) {
+            dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] = current[localColumn][localRow];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+// Phase 3: all non-pivot tiles are independent. The two input tiles are staged
+// once in shared memory and each thread keeps multiple outputs in registers.
+template <unsigned int BLOCK_ROWS, unsigned int MIN_BLOCKS_PER_SM>
+__global__ __launch_bounds__(TILE_SIZE * BLOCK_ROWS, MIN_BLOCKS_PER_SM)
+void updateRemainingTiles(unsigned int* __restrict__ dist,
+                          const unsigned int n, const unsigned int round) {
+    __shared__ unsigned int toPivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+    __shared__ unsigned int fromPivot[TILE_SIZE][TILE_SIZE];
+
+    const unsigned int compactRowTile = blockIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int compactColumnTile = blockIdx.y;
+    const unsigned int rowTile =
+        compactRowTile + (compactRowTile >= round);
+    const unsigned int columnTile =
+        compactColumnTile + (compactColumnTile >= round);
+    const unsigned int localRow = threadIdx.x;
+    const unsigned int row = rowTile * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
+    const unsigned int pivotRow = round * TILE_SIZE + localRow;
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y; localColumn < TILE_SIZE;
+         localColumn += BLOCK_ROWS) {
+        const unsigned int pivotColumn = round * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        toPivot[localColumn][localRow] =
+            (row < n && pivotColumn < n)
+                ? dist[matrixIndex(row, pivotColumn, n)]
+                : INF;
+        fromPivot[localColumn][localRow] =
+            (pivotRow < n && column < n)
+                ? dist[matrixIndex(pivotRow, column, n)]
+                : INF;
+    }
+    __syncthreads();
+
+    unsigned int values[TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS];
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int item = 0; item < TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS; ++item) {
+        const unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y + item * BLOCK_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        values[item] = (row < n && column < n)
+                           ? dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)]
+                           : INF;
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int k = 0; k < TILE_SIZE; ++k) {
+        const unsigned int distanceToK = toPivot[k][localRow];
+#pragma unroll
+        for (unsigned int item = 0; item < TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS; ++item) {
+            const unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y + item * BLOCK_ROWS;
+            const unsigned int throughK =
+                distanceToK + fromPivot[localColumn][k];
+            if (throughK < values[item]) {
+                values[item] = throughK;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+#pragma unroll
+    for (unsigned int item = 0; item < TILE_SIZE / BLOCK_ROWS; ++item) {
+        const unsigned int localColumn = threadIdx.y + item * BLOCK_ROWS;
+        const unsigned int column = columnTile * TILE_SIZE + localColumn;
+        if (row < n && column < n) {
+            dist[matrixIndex(row, column, n)] = values[item];
+        }
+    }
+}
+
+void floydWarshall(unsigned int* const deviceDist, const unsigned int numNodes) {
+    if (numNodes == 0) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const unsigned int tileCount =
+        (numNodes + TILE_SIZE - 1) / TILE_SIZE;
+    const dim3 phase12Threads(TILE_SIZE, PHASE12_BLOCK_ROWS);
+
+    for (unsigned int round = 0; round < tileCount; ++round) {
+        updatePivotTile<<<1, phase12Threads>>>(deviceDist, numNodes, round);
+
+        if (tileCount > 1) {
+            updatePivotRowAndColumn<<<2 * (tileCount - 1), phase12Threads>>>(
+                deviceDist, numNodes, round, tileCount);
+            const dim3 remainingTiles(tileCount - 1, tileCount - 1);
+            if (tileCount <= SMALL_GRAPH_TILE_LIMIT) {
+                const dim3 phase3Threads(TILE_SIZE, SMALL_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS);
+                updateRemainingTiles<SMALL_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS, 3>
+                    <<<remainingTiles, phase3Threads>>>(deviceDist, numNodes,
+                                                        round);
+            } else {
+                const dim3 phase3Threads(TILE_SIZE, LARGE_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS);
+                updateRemainingTiles<LARGE_PHASE3_BLOCK_ROWS, 4>
+                    <<<remainingTiles, phase3Threads>>>(deviceDist, numNodes,
+                                                        round);
+            }
         }
     }
+
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());
 }

Implemented an unconditional CUDA version while preserving the `floydwarshall` executable name and adding no files or dependencies.

Key changes:

- Three-phase blocked Floyd–Warshall with 32×32 shared-memory tiles.
- Coalesced column-major memory access and register-resident outputs.
- Adaptive launch geometry for small and large graphs.
- Native GPU architecture optimization.
- CUDA allocation, synchronization, transfer, and error handling.
- Handles partial tiles and empty graphs correctly.
